function	timestamp_id(table_name text)	{"schema" : "public", "name" : "timestamp_id", "identity_arguments" : "table_name text", "result" : "bigint", "language" : "plpgsql", "kind" : "f", "volatility" : "v", "parallel" : "u", "security_definer" : false, "leakproof" : false, "strict" : false, "definition" : "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.timestamp_id(table_name text)\n RETURNS bigint\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\nAS $function$\n  DECLARE\n    time_part bigint;\n    sequence_base bigint;\n    tail bigint;\n  BEGIN\n    time_part := (((date_part('epoch', now()) * 1000))::bigint << 16);\n    sequence_base := (\n      'x' || substr(\n        md5(table_name || 'rustodon-mastodon-v4.6.5-fixture-salt' || time_part::text),\n        1,\n        4\n      )\n    )::bit(16)::bigint;\n    tail := ((sequence_base + nextval(table_name || '_id_seq')) & 65535);\n    RETURN time_part | tail;\n  END\n$function$\n"}

materialized_view	account_summaries	{"schema" : "public", "name" : "account_summaries", "definition" : " SELECT accounts.id AS account_id,\n    mode() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY t0.language) AS language,\n    mode() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY t0.sensitive) AS sensitive\n   FROM accounts\n     CROSS JOIN LATERAL ( SELECT statuses.account_id,\n            statuses.language,\n            statuses.sensitive\n           FROM statuses\n          WHERE statuses.account_id = accounts.id AND statuses.deleted_at IS NULL AND statuses.reblog_of_id IS NULL\n          ORDER BY statuses.id DESC\n         LIMIT 20) t0\n  WHERE accounts.suspended_at IS NULL AND accounts.silenced_at IS NULL AND accounts.moved_to_account_id IS NULL AND accounts.discoverable = true AND accounts.locked = false\n  GROUP BY accounts.id;"}
materialized_view	global_follow_recommendations	{"schema" : "public", "name" : "global_follow_recommendations", "definition" : " SELECT t0.account_id,\n    sum(t0.rank) AS rank,\n    array_agg(t0.reason) AS reason\n   FROM ( SELECT account_summaries.account_id,\n            count(follows.id)::numeric / (1.0 + count(follows.id)::numeric) AS rank,\n            'most_followed'::text AS reason\n           FROM follows\n             JOIN account_summaries ON account_summaries.account_id = follows.target_account_id\n             JOIN users ON users.account_id = follows.account_id\n          WHERE users.current_sign_in_at >= (now() - '30 days'::interval) AND account_summaries.sensitive = false AND NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1\n                   FROM follow_recommendation_suppressions\n                  WHERE follow_recommendation_suppressions.account_id = follows.target_account_id))\n          GROUP BY account_summaries.account_id\n         HAVING count(follows.id) >= 5\n        UNION ALL\n         SELECT account_summaries.account_id,\n            sum(status_stats.reblogs_count + status_stats.favourites_count) / (1.0 + sum(status_stats.reblogs_count + status_stats.favourites_count)) AS rank,\n            'most_interactions'::text AS reason\n           FROM status_stats\n             JOIN statuses ON statuses.id = status_stats.status_id\n             JOIN account_summaries ON account_summaries.account_id = statuses.account_id\n          WHERE statuses.id >= ((date_part('epoch'::text, now() - '30 days'::interval) * 1000::double precision)::bigint << 16) AND account_summaries.sensitive = false AND NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1\n                   FROM follow_recommendation_suppressions\n                  WHERE follow_recommendation_suppressions.account_id = statuses.account_id))\n          GROUP BY account_summaries.account_id\n         HAVING sum(status_stats.reblogs_count + status_stats.favourites_count) >= 5::numeric) t0\n  GROUP BY t0.account_id\n  ORDER BY (sum(t0.rank)) DESC;"}
materialized_view	instances	{"schema" : "public", "name" : "instances", "definition" : " WITH domain_counts(domain, accounts_count) AS (\n         SELECT accounts.domain,\n            count(*) AS accounts_count\n           FROM accounts\n          WHERE accounts.domain IS NOT NULL\n          GROUP BY accounts.domain\n        )\n SELECT domain_counts.domain,\n    domain_counts.accounts_count\n   FROM domain_counts\nUNION\n SELECT domain_blocks.domain,\n    COALESCE(domain_counts.accounts_count, 0::bigint) AS accounts_count\n   FROM domain_blocks\n     LEFT JOIN domain_counts ON domain_counts.domain::text = domain_blocks.domain::text\nUNION\n SELECT domain_allows.domain,\n    COALESCE(domain_counts.accounts_count, 0::bigint) AS accounts_count\n   FROM domain_allows\n     LEFT JOIN domain_counts ON domain_counts.domain::text = domain_allows.domain::text;"}

view	user_ips	{"schema" : "public", "name" : "user_ips", "definition" : " SELECT t0.user_id,\n    t0.ip,\n    max(t0.used_at) AS used_at\n   FROM ( SELECT users.id AS user_id,\n            users.sign_up_ip AS ip,\n            users.created_at AS used_at\n           FROM users\n          WHERE users.sign_up_ip IS NOT NULL\n        UNION ALL\n         SELECT session_activations.user_id,\n            session_activations.ip,\n            session_activations.updated_at\n           FROM session_activations\n        UNION ALL\n         SELECT login_activities.user_id,\n            login_activities.ip,\n            login_activities.created_at\n           FROM login_activities\n          WHERE login_activities.success = true) t0\n  GROUP BY t0.user_id, t0.ip;"}
